
GER_rksplus_category_generalboard = {

	GER_reichskommissariat_city_nordstern = {
	
		icon = generic_construction

		available = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_uplift_the_rosenberg_office
				has_country_flag = GER_rksplus_startup_event_enable
			}
			rkplus_ger_can_run_rks = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			    tooltip = rksplus.tooltip.185
				143  = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }

			}

			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 15
			
		}

			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 143
			}
		}

		cost = 300
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}

		visible = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_uplift_the_rosenberg_office
				has_country_flag = GER_rksplus_startup_event_enable
			}
			rkplus_ger_can_run_rks = yes
			
		}
		
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = rksplus.tooltip.186
					143  = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }

				}
			}
		}
		
		days_remove = 1095
		
		fire_only_once = yes

		remove_effect = {
			country_event = rksplus.125
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_timed_idea = { idea = GER_rksplus_nordstern_idea days = 1095 }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			country_event = rksplus.126
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_ideas = GER_rksplus_nordstern_idea
			}
		}
	}

	# Send more soldiers to man the RKs 
	GER_wehrbauern_easternrks_rksplus = {

    GER_rksplus_unite_nordafrika = {

		icon = GFX_decision_hol_draw_up_staff_plans

		available = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_uplift_the_rosenberg_office

			    tooltip = rksplus.unitetooltip.1
